<div class="ww-panel-heading ww-panel-heavy">
<div class="ww-icon-header ww-icon-header--bottom">
<svg aria-labelledby="title" class="icon icon-question-circle" focusable="false" id="teaser-question-circle-home" role="img"><use xlink:href="#icon-question-circle"></use></svg>
<h2 class="ww-h2 ww-icon-header-content">Frequently Asked Questions</h2>
</div>
</div>
<div class="ww-panel-body">
<div class="ww-panel-subheading text-uppercase">What is a Health Care FSA?</div>
<p>
                    A Health Care FSA (HCFSA) is a pre-tax benefit account that's used to pay for eligible medical, dental, and
                    vision care expenses that are not covered by your health care plan or elsewhere. With an HCFSA, you use
                    pre-tax dollars to pay for qualified out-of-pocket health care expenses.
                </p>
</div>
<div class="ww-fill"></div>
<a class="ww-panel-footer ww-button ww-button--block text-uppercase" href="/support/faq"><span>View all FAQs</span> <svg aria-labelledby="title" class="icon icon-dot-arrow" focusable="false" id="teaser-faq-dot-arrow" role="img"><use xlink:href="#icon-dot-arrow"></use></svg></a>
</div>
</div>
<div class="ww-panel ww-panel--green ww-panel--standalone">
<div class="ww-panel-container">
<div class="ww-panel-heading ww-panel-heavy">
<div class="ww-icon-header ww-icon-header--bottom">
<svg aria-labelledby="title" class="icon icon-calculator" focusable="false" id="teaser-calculator-home" role="img"><use xlink:href="#icon-calculator"></use></svg>
<h2 class="ww-h2 ww-icon-header-content">Savings Calculators</h2>
</div>
</div>
<div class="ww-panel-body">
<p>Our calculators will help you estimate your Flexible Spending Account contributions and potential annual savings. Your tax savings are based on marginal federal/state rates and FICA.</p>
</div>
<div class="ww-fill"></div>
<a class="ww-panel-footer ww-button ww-button--block text-uppercase" href="/support/savingscalculators"><span>FSAFEDS Savings Calculators</span> <svg aria-labelledby="title" class="icon icon-dot-arrow" focusable="false" id="teaser-calc-dot-arrow" role="img"><use xlink:href="#icon-dot-arrow"></use></svg></a>

<p>
                        Be sure you can access your FSAFEDS account before Open Season enrollment starts on Monday, November 11, 2019.  Verifying your account login now will save time when you're ready to make your 2020 elections.
